- **CRE-017** · **Low** · Player-visible — **Mephit breath weapons and most creature SLAs are text only.** `MephitBase` sets no `BreathWeapon` or spells and models fast healing 2 as `RegenerationAmount = 2` (that code acts like fast healing), and about 30-35 "spell-like" mentions across 13 creature files (hags, slaadi, yuan-ti and others) are description only. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase_M.cs:89-148; Assets/Scripts/Character/Controller/CharacterController.cs:4854, 5227. *Fix:* give mephits `BreathWeaponDefinition` entries (already used by Hell Hounds and dragons) and add a FastHealing field.
- **CRE-018** · **Low** — **Creature trample (and constrict) is not implemented.** `NPCDefinition.HasTrample` is set only for the Treant and never read, and creature trample is SpecialAbilities text only (Treant, giant beetles, Gorgon, wild-shape forms); `FeatManager.HasTrample` and `MountedCombatSystem` implement the unrelated mounted Trample feat. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase.cs:612; Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase_T.cs:180; Assets/Scripts/Character/Feats/FeatManager.cs:975. *Fix:* implement a creature trample action that uses the field, or remove it. Constrict is text only too (RULES_COVERAGE 3.2, "Constrict, swallow whole, trample"); when it is built, follow the delegated RAW reading of 2026-10-14 (RULES_COVERAGE 4.1, a 3-0 panel; MM p.307 "in addition", MM p.310 "as well"): the constriction damage is dealt on the successful hold and on every later successful grapple check, on top of the hold damage of the attack that established it, the creature entry setting only the amount (an otyugh's 1d6 tentacle plus 1d6 constriction; a constrictor snake's bite plus its constriction).
- **CRE-019** · **Low** — **Summon tables stop at level IV.** `SummonMonsterLists` has Summon Monster and Summon Nature's Ally tables for I-IV only (SM V is empty); harmless today because only levels 1-4 of both spells exist, and the stub staff spell `summon_monster_ix` would not match the `summon_monster_(\d+)` regex anyway. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Spell/Components/SummonMonsterLists.cs:96-124; Assets/Scripts/Equipment/Staves/StaffDatabase.cs:657. *Fix:* add SM and SNA V+ tables before adding those spells, and use a numeric id for any staff that casts SM IX.
- **CRE-020** · **Low** — **The DMG NPC-by-level system has no entry point.** `QuickSpawnSystem`, `NPCTemplateDatabase`, `TemplateData` (70 templates) and 2 more classes are called only from NPCTemplateSystemTests. Only Wizard 5 fills `SpellsPrepared`, and `NPCTemplateAIConfigurator.ConfigureConsumables` maps potions, scrolls and wands but never weapons or armor. The templates' stored scores and hit points are not audited against the delegated RAW reading of 2026-10-14 (CRE-058, RULES_COVERAGE 4.1): the eleven PC-class templates are elite DMG chapter 4 NPCs (maximum first Hit Die, elite array as the class tables arrange it) and an NPC-class template is average with the nonelite array. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Utilities/QuickSpawnSystem.cs:18-131; Assets/Scripts/AI/NPCTemplateAIConfigurator.cs:40-43, 124-145. *Fix:* audit `NPCTemplateDatabase` (PC-class rows maximized and elite, NPC-class rows averaged and nonelite); wire QuickSpawnSystem into encounter generation with weapon and armor mapping, or mark it experimental.
- **CRE-021** · **Low** · Player-visible — **Summon Nature's Ally lists lack 6 aquatic creatures (45/51 entries).** SNA I-II lack Porpoise, Medium Shark and Squid, and SNA IV lacks Sea Cat, Huge Shark and Juvenile Tojanida, all for want of an NPCDatabase entry. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Spell/Components/SummonMonsterLists.cs:494, 517, 519, 568, 569, 572. *Fix:* add the NPCDatabase entries.
- **CRE-030** · **Med** · Player-visible — **Vampire spell list is unusable because it is a Fighter.** The vampire definition is `CharacterClass = "Fighter"`, so `stats.IsSpellcaster` is false and `InitializeNPCFromDefinition` never adds a `SpellcastingComponent` despite its `KnownSpellIds`. The melee routines try a spell first since batch 13 step 8 (AI-002, fixed 2026-10-29), so the vampire would cast once it has spellcasting; the class is what stops it. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase_V.cs:261, 298; Assets/Scripts/_Core/GameManager.NPCSetup.cs:1114, 1206-1211. *Fix:* Give the vampire its MM spell-like abilities (or a caster class) instead of a Fighter spell list.
- **CRE-031** · **Med** · Player-visible — **Stench auras and grapple blood drain are configured but never used.** `ConfigureStenchAura` stores a DC and range (set for creatures in NPCDatabase_G, _N and _T) and `ConfigureBloodDrain` stores a `BloodDrainDefinition`, but `HasStenchAura` and `GetBloodDrainDefinition` have no readers. No stench save or grapple CON drain ever happens; only the separate natural-attack blood drain works. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Controller/CharacterController.cs:624-626, 641-643, 4907-4930; Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase_G.cs:540. *Fix:* Process stench as an aura at turn start and apply blood drain while grappling, or remove the fields.
- **CRE-032** · **Low** · Player-visible — **Summon command menu has no effect; ProtectCaster is unreachable.** The command menu (AttackNearest/ProtectCaster) is offered only for controllable summons, which take player turns, while `CurrentCommand` is read only by the AI summon turn. AI summons always keep the default AttackNearest, so ProtectCaster never applies. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/_Core/GameManager.cs:2494-2530; Assets/Scripts/Spell/Resolution/GameManager.SpellCasting.cs:542; Assets/Scripts/_Core/GameManager.NPCTurns.cs:99, 190-197. *Fix:* Either offer the menu for AI-controlled player summons or remove it for controllable ones.
- **CRE-037** · **Med** · Player-visible — **Energy riders on natural attacks are data only.** Seven creature files set `BonusElementalDamageDice/Count/Type` on `NaturalAttackDefinition` entries (for example the nightmare's flaming hooves; the hell hound's fiery bite is not even in its attack data, only SpecialAbilities text), but no damage code reads them (only the AI's threat estimate, `AiCastChoiceHeuristics`, does); the only other writer is `UndeadTemplateUtils`, which zeroes them. So these attacks deal physical damage only. Noted in `docs/systems/CREATURES.md` without an ID until now. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Stats/CharacterStats.cs:83-85, 216-218; Assets/Scripts/Character/Templates/UndeadTemplateUtils.cs:27-28; Assets/Scripts/AI/Spellcasting/AiCastChoiceHeuristics.cs:79-81; Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase_N.cs:46; Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase_H.cs:137; `grep -rn BonusElementalDamage Assets/Scripts`. *Fix:* add the rider as a typed damage packet in the natural-attack damage path (through `ApplyIncomingDamage` so resistances apply).

## Development traps

- **CRE-022** · **Med** · Player-visible — **Creature IDs silently overwrite each other.** `NPCDatabase.Register` has no duplicate check and `Init` registers files in a hand-kept, non-alphabetical order, so the later entry wins in 5 collisions (dretch, skeleton_archer and 3 more). Two creatures also exist under two IDs used by different consumers (large_viper/viper_large, huge_monstrous_centipede/monstrous_centipede_huge). The `dretch` that wins (`RegisterDretch`) has Str 14 where MM p.42 gives 12 (the losing `RegisterSummonDretch` has 12), so every dretch, summoned or encountered, gets +1 to melee attack and damage rolls; SummonMonster3CreaturesTests fails on it (2026-10-07). *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase.cs:18-62, 212-226; Assets/Scripts/Spell/Components/SummonMonsterLists.cs:200, 542. *Fix:* warn on duplicate IDs, delete or rename the losers, and merge each double-ID creature into one ID or an explicit alias.
- **CRE-023** · **Med** — **Template adapters and `NPCDefinition.Clone` copy hand-picked fields.** The skeleton, zombie and lycanthrope adapters copy back only about 60 fields, losing the factories' clearing of auras and regeneration (latent today; since batch 12 step 2, 2026-10-28, they copy the breath weapons, frightful presence and engulf, which the factories clear). Clone is partly shallow and its SwarmTraits copy drops `SwarmDamageCount`; a new NPCDefinition field must be added to Clone, three `CopyDefinitionFields` methods and the factories. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Templates/SkeletonCreatureTemplate.cs:41-126; Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase.cs:910-1034. *Fix:* have the adapters replace the definition wholesale and make Clone deep-copy reference fields.
- **CRE-024** · **Med** — **Monsters carry placeholder class levels.** 250 database entries use `CharacterClass = "Warrior"` for racial HD, which becomes a real Warrior level, so `IsWarrior` and `HasClass("Warrior")` are true for monsters (no production reader yet). Weapon and armor proficiency already tells the two apart (CHR-072, 2026-10-08): `NPCDefinition.ClassLevelsAreRacialHitDice` (derived: an empty class, or Warrior on a non-humanoid; set explicitly for the gnoll, bugbear, lizardfolk and troglodyte, humanoids with more than 1 racial HD, MM p.310) becomes `CharacterStats.RacialHitDiceStandInClass`, which the proficiency tables ignore; that marker could serve the rest of this fix. Skeletons and zombies get Fighter 1 from `EnsureMulticlassDataInitialized`, so `IsFighter`, `AISpellcastingStrategist.IsFrontliner` and WishExecutor treat them as fighters. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Stats/CharacterStats.cs:468-476, 2322-2330, 2665, 6386-6391; Assets/Scripts/Character/Templates/SkeletonTemplate.cs:90. Since CRE-004 (2026-10-08) BAB and base saves also skip the stand-in class (`CharacterStats.RacialHitDice` takes its levels with the type's progressions). *Fix:* use an explicit racial-HD marker for the class checks too, or skip the Fighter fallback when `RacialHitDiceStandInClass` is set.
- **CRE-025** · **Low** — **The creature count is only known at runtime.** `NPCDatabase.Init` registers about 30 partial files, generates dragons in a loop and adds aliases, so the authoritative number is the "[NPCDatabase] Initialized with N NPC types." log line. A static count (about 317 `Register(` calls, up to 60 dragons, plus aliases) suggests about 380-390 (not verified in Play mode). *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase.cs:20-61. *Fix:* quote the runtime log line instead of hard-coding a count in docs or tests.
- **CRE-033** · **Low** — **Creature type and tag spell "Magical Beast" two ways.** 26 definitions use `CreatureType = "Magical Beast"` and 4 use `"MagicalBeast"` (plus `BaseCreatureDefinitions`); `CreatureTags` mix both too. `CreatureTypeProgression` accepts both, and so does the ranger's favored enemy matching since 2026-10-14 (`RangerRules.MatchesFavoredEnemy` ignores spacing and case), but other exact-string checks and tag matching miss the other spelling. Runtime count via `NPCDatabase.AllNPCs` on 2026-10-03. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase_C.cs:614, NPCDatabase_H.cs:766/820/874; Assets/Scripts/Character/Templates/BaseCreatureDefinitions.cs:30; Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/CreatureTypeProgression.cs:111-112. *Fix:* normalise all data to "Magical Beast" (or compare via `CreatureTypeProgression`'s alias map).

## Dead code, duplication and tech debt

- **CRE-026** · **Low** — **Creature data is about 15.4k lines of hand-written C# with no validator.** There are no data assets and no database validator (largest: NPCDatabaseCustom.cs 1698, DragonData.cs 1601, NPCDatabase_G.cs 1365 lines), so stat errors surface only at runtime or in a few hand-run tests. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase*.cs. *Fix:* add a static validator over all NPCDefinitions (HD vs HP, BAB vs progression, CR parse, attacks) or migrate to data assets.
- **CRE-027** · **Low** — **Four CR-string parsers.** `CRCalculator.CRToFloat` (culture-sensitive; `FloatToCR` has no 1/10), `ChallengeRatingUtils.TryParse`, `CustomEncounterBuilderUI.ParseCRToFloat` and a private `LycanthropeTemplate.ParseCR`. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/CreatureClass/CRCalculator.cs:23-50; Assets/Scripts/Character/Creatures/NPCDatabase.cs:1149-1230. *Fix:* keep ChallengeRatingUtils and delete the others.
- **CRE-028** · **Low** — **`StatArrayApplier`'s dictionary helpers are test-only.** Since 2026-10-14 (CRE-058) live spawns use `StatArrayApplier.ArrangeByPriority`, `TryGetDmgNpcStartingScores`, `DmgNpcIncreaseAbility` and `TryGetRacialAdjustments` through `CreatureClassEngine.ApplyClassToDefinition`. `ApplyArray`, `ApplyRacialModifiers` and `ApplyAbilityIncreases` are still referenced only by NPCTemplateSystemTests, and `ApplyAbilityIncreases` puts every increase on the class's first priority score, which the DMG chapter 4 tables do not (the monk raises Dex and Wis in turn). *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/CreatureClass/StatArrayApplier.cs (`ApplyArray`, `ApplyRacialModifiers`, `ApplyAbilityIncreases`); Assets/Scripts/Tests/Classes/NPCTemplateSystemTests.cs. *Fix:* delete the three helpers and move their tests to the live methods.
- **CRE-029** · **Low** — **Unused CR and class-engine methods.** `CRCalculator.CalculateNPCCR`, `GetStandardCR`, `FloatToCR` and five `CreatureClassEngine` methods (`CalculateClassSave`, `CalculateTotalBAB` and 3 more) are test-only; `CalculateTotalCR` has no callers and `CalculateTotalSave` only a test caller. *Evidence:* Assets/Scripts/Character/CreatureClass/CRCalculator.cs:23-122; Assets/Scripts/Character/CreatureClass/CreatureClassEngine.cs:43-150. *Fix:* delete them or use them for class-leveled NPCs.
